# Evidence Log — the AUTOPILOT self-study

> Append-only chronology, newest last. Moved out of `PAPER.md`'s reading path on 2026-09-04 (operator ask: the
> paper needed clear conclusions up front, not ~2,100 lines of raw log between the reader and the analysis).
> The flight-end ritual (`scripts/self-study/generate-data.mjs`) appends its dated entries HERE; `PAPER.md` keeps
> the abstract, method, data tables, and discussion. Same conventions as before the move: dated bullets, never
> rewritten, machine entries marked as such.

## Evidence Log

Append-only, dated, newest last. Two kinds of entry land here: hand-written ones, added when something about the
study itself changes (a new section, a corrected error, a new source of data), and automated ones ("Automated flight
update"), appended by `scripts/self-study/generate-data.mjs` at the end of any flight that shipped ≥1 firing — see
§7. That automated cadence is the "SELF-STUDY updater" slice this note used to describe as not-yet-built.

- **2026-08-10** — Foundation laid: this document's skeleton, the `DATA:SUMMARY` auto-generation script, and the
  `pnpm self-study:update` command. §5 (Discussion) is a placeholder; no independent evaluation of the self-reported
  fields has been done yet (see §6).
- **2026-08-11** — New data source: §4 gained a Firing-Prompt-Version row, resolved from git commit trailers rather
  than the `metrics` table (that field was never captured there — see §3). §7 updated to disclose the new source.
- **2026-08-11** — Automation landed (the "SELF-STUDY updater" slice): `apps/dashboard/src/fly.ts` now runs
  `generate-data.mjs` at the end of any flight that shipped ≥1 firing, which both refreshes §4 and appends one
  "Automated flight update" entry below this one — the cadence §8's intro paragraph and §7 used to describe as
  not-yet-built. A plain `pnpm self-study:update` run by hand still only refreshes §4, unchanged from before.
- **2026-08-11** — First slice of SOTA-MAP H3 ("build your own eval"): §4 gained an "Eval regression by prompt
  version" table, grouping every firing (not only shipped ones) by the `promptVersion` the engine records at run
  time and reporting pass rate, cost variance, median turns, and cost per solved task together, per version — the
  four numbers H3 asks for so a prompt-version bump can be judged on evidence instead of vibes. On this run it
  already shows the pattern the change is meant to surface: `firing-v8.1` passes 94.0% vs. `firing-v7`'s 70.6%, at
  lower cost per solved task. Still a slice, not the full item — this reads AUTOPILOT's own firing history as an ad
  hoc eval set; it does not yet define a fixed, replayable task suite with pre-registered known-good outcomes that a
  candidate prompt version is run against *before* it ships, which is what "gate prompt-version bumps on" ultimately
  requires. §6 and §7 updated to disclose the new source.
- **2026-08-11** — Second slice of SOTA-MAP H3: the pool is now pinned, not just grouped. `verifiedKnownGoodFirings`
  and `evalRegressionOverPinnedSuite` (`packages/store/src/read.ts`) let `scripts/self-study/pin-eval-suite.mjs`
  freeze 50 mechanically-verified-good firings into a committed `docs/SELF-STUDY/eval-suite.json` (refuses to
  overwrite without `--force`), and §4 gained a second table reporting the same four H3 numbers over only that fixed
  set. This directly fixes the "not yet a fixed... task suite with pre-registered known-good outcomes" gap the
  previous entry disclosed — the population these numbers describe can no longer silently grow between two readings
  of this document. It does not fix the harder gap: these are already-shipped historical tasks, not a suite a
  candidate prompt version can be run against before shipping (real replay). All 50 pinned firings happen to be
  `firing-v8.1` (§4), since that is what the 50 most recent known-good firings ran under — the pinned suite has no
  `firing-v7`/`firing-v8` coverage yet. §6 and §7 updated to disclose the new mechanism and its remaining gap.
- **2026-08-11** — First slice of the human-vs-agent evaluation (backlog web-msniol15-foo6oi): §4 gained an
  "Outcomes by task pick source" table, grouping firings by `operator-assigned` / `self-proposed` / `free-pick` /
  `untracked-item` via a new `evalRegressionByPickSource` (`packages/store/src/read.ts`), joining the existing
  `metrics.item` and `tasks.source` columns — no new capture pipeline needed. This is only the "compare
  operator-assigned vs self-picked task outcomes" half of the backlog item; it does not capture operator
  approvals, rejections, edits, or corrections as evaluation labels (the harder half), which remain unbuilt — no
  such events are recorded anywhere in the store yet. §3, §6, and §7 updated to disclose the new source and its
  limits as a proxy for guidance quality.
- **2026-08-11** — TDD-first-for-bug-tasks mechanism landed (backlog web-msnsxuep-ytwucr): the firing prompt
  (`firing-v8.3`) now requires a FAILING test reproducing the bug BEFORE the fix on every `kind:"fix"` firing, and
  the agent self-reports compliance via a new `METRICS.testFirst` field, persisted as `metrics.test_first` (schema
  v9) and aggregated by a new `testFirstCompliance` (`packages/store/src/read.ts`) into a §4 table. Zero historical
  firings predate this field, so on this run every existing `kind:"fix"` firing reports `NULL` (not reported) —
  the compliance rate has no data yet and will only start reflecting real behavior from the next `kind:"fix"`
  firing onward. §3, §6, and §7 updated to disclose the new source and that it is self-reported, not (yet)
  independently verified.
- **2026-08-11** — Automated flight update: 19 firing(s) this flight (18 shipped), 299 total recorded.
- **2026-08-12** — Chart data plane added (backlog web-msnsgcvf-zgmo7i): §4 gained a `DATA:SERIES` block, a
  machine-readable JSON payload alongside the human-readable tables — per-firing rows (cost, turns, outcome,
  prompt version), per-day aggregates, and a per-era comparison (reusing the existing prompt-version eval rollup).
  A new `firingSeries` (`packages/store/src/read.ts`) supplies the per-firing join; `generate-data.mjs` shapes and
  writes it. No chart consumes this yet — this slice is the data plane only. §7 updated to disclose the new block.
- **2026-08-12** — §5 (Discussion) filled in (backlog web-msnshjvp-dtlnoc): replaced the placeholder with an
  era-segmented reading of §4's existing per-day and per-`Firing-Prompt-Version` tables against the three RQs in
  §2 — no new analysis code, no model call, same sourcing §7 already discloses for those tables. Notable finding:
  RQ3 (efficiency evolution) does NOT show a clean "later prompt versions got cheaper" trend — cost and median
  turns per shipped firing both bottom out at `firing-v8.1` and rise in every era after it, the opposite of the
  intuitive story. 🟣 flagged inline as needing human confirmation before it drives any prompt-version decision.

- **2026-08-20** — Third slice of the human-vs-agent evaluation (backlog web-msniol15-foo6oi): the "capture
  operator approvals, rejections, edits, or corrections as evaluation labels" half was already half-built — a
  prior slice had `mutate.ts`'s `setTaskStatus`/`deleteTask` write `'evaluation-label'` events on every
  approve/reject verdict the operator makes on a self-proposed task, but nothing ever read them back. This entry
  closes that gap: a new `evaluationLabelSummary` (`packages/store/src/read.ts`) aggregates those events into
  approved/rejected counts and an approval rate, and `generate-data.mjs` renders it as a new §4 table ("Evaluation
  labels") next to the pick-source table. It still does not capture edits or corrections, or verdicts on
  operator-assigned/already-approved tasks — only approve/reject on the agent's OWN self-proposed ones, per
  `recordEvaluationLabel`'s scoping. The table stays empty until the store records its first real verdict (same
  "no data until it happens" convention as the TDD-first table) — this firing's worktree has no reachable
  telemetry store to check against (`.autopilot/autopilot.db` is git-ignored local runtime state), so whether it
  already has data is confirmed only by the next real `pnpm self-study:update` regeneration. §4, §6, and §7
  updated to disclose the new table and its remaining scope gap.

- **2026-08-21** — Fifth slice of the human-vs-agent evaluation (backlog J / web-msniol15-foo6oi): starts the
  evolution-over-time view (checkbox 5), the one checkbox the fourth slice confirmed had no data plane at all.
  `evaluationLabelDayCounts` (`packages/store/src/read.ts`) buckets every `'evaluation-label'` event's
  approve/reject verdict by UTC calendar day, mirroring `firingDayCounts`'s day-bucketing convention so the same
  chart-axis math already proven for the contribution heatmap applies here — TDD (RED: 5 failing tests against a
  function that did not exist; GREEN: `json_valid(payload)` had to gate `json_extract` in the WHERE clause once the
  malformed-payload test caught SQLite throwing on invalid JSON rather than returning NULL, unlike
  `evaluationLabelSummary`'s JS-side try/catch). This is query-layer only: no API route or dashboard panel reads it
  yet, so `approval-rate ↑ / rejection-rate ↓ ... over time` is still not visible to the operator anywhere — checkbox
  5 moves from open to partial in `docs/BACKLOG-999.md`, not to done. Per the UX-EXPRESSION DOCTRINE this remains a
  slice, and per backlog J's own scope it is one increment of a still-multi-firing task, not a claim that the
  feature ships.
  - [View the previous version on GitHub](https://github.com/M-A-S-T-E-R-M-I-N-D/AUTOPILOT/blob/f286a54/docs/SELF-STUDY/PAPER.md)
- **2026-08-21** — Sixth slice of the human-vs-agent evaluation (backlog J / web-msniol15-foo6oi): the fifth slice's
  `evaluationLabelDayCounts` query now travels one hop closer to the operator — the dashboard's DB gather seam
  (`apps/dashboard/src/read/source.ts`) calls it for every project and `ProjectAggregate`
  (`apps/dashboard/src/read/fleet.ts`) carries the result as an optional field, mirroring exactly how
  `firingDayCounts` reaches the contribution heatmap (optional-so-fixtures-type-check convention, same as
  `warmSessions`/`orientLengths`). No new query logic and no new tests: the store function keeps its five TDD tests
  from the fifth slice, and the dashboard payload wiring follows the untested-by-convention seam every sibling
  field uses. Still no panel renders it, so checkbox 5 stays partial in `docs/BACKLOG-999.md` and per the
  UX-EXPRESSION DOCTRINE this remains a slice — the remaining work is now purely a `web/` renderer.
  - [View the previous version on GitHub](https://github.com/M-A-S-T-E-R-M-I-N-D/AUTOPILOT/blob/8f0ea91/docs/SELF-STUDY/PAPER.md)
- **2026-08-21** — Seventh slice of the human-vs-agent evaluation (backlog J / web-msniol15-foo6oi): the
  evolution view's pure trend math now exists as `apps/dashboard/src/web/evaluation-trend.ts` — Sun-start weekly
  buckets of the served `evaluationLabelDayCounts` (fixed 12-week window, verdict-free weeks render as gaps, not
  fake 0%), a whole-window summary whose improving/declining direction compares the later half of
  verdict-carrying weeks against the earlier half behind a ±5-point dead band (a single noisy week must never
  read as "declining"), and the aria-label text. TDD-first: 15 tests written RED against the missing module,
  then the implementation, following the `heatmap.ts`/`sparkline.ts` self-contained DOM-free convention. The
  fleet dimension decided the slice boundary: `web/shell.ts` — the only wiring point that makes a panel visible —
  was claimed by two sibling flights this firing, so the shell embed + panel markup is deliberately deferred
  rather than colliding. Checkbox 5 stays partial and per the UX-EXPRESSION DOCTRINE this remains a slice — the
  remaining work is the one-panel shell wiring once that file frees up.

- **2026-08-21** — Eighth slice of the human-vs-agent evaluation (backlog J / web-msniol15-foo6oi), and a live
  specimen of fleet claims cutting a unit at commit time: the firing's starting FLEET snapshot showed
  `web/shell.ts` unclaimed, so the full panel wiring was built and gate-green — then the PRE-COMMIT SIBLING SCAN
  (a fresh re-check, not the stale snapshot) found a sibling flight claiming the file and the commit was blocked.
  The unit was reshaped at the nearest coherent boundary instead of shipped whole: `evaluation-trend.ts` is now
  fully embed-ready (day/week helpers and `EVAL_TREND_DAY_MS`/`EVAL_TREND_WEEK_MS` exported for the shell's
  JSON.stringify()/.toString() no-drift convention, plus `evaluationTrendWeekTip` for per-bar labels, tested),
  the panel's CSS landed in `layout-css.ts`, and the four DOM specs that boot the real shell are committed
  `describe.skip` at the cut with a comment naming why — so the remaining diff for the next firing is
  shell-only: embed, append after the heatmap, unskip. Checkbox 5 stays `[~]` in `docs/BACKLOG-999.md`; nothing
  is visible to the operator yet, which is exactly what the UX-EXPRESSION DOCTRINE says a slice is.
  - [View the previous version on GitHub](https://github.com/M-A-S-T-E-R-M-I-N-D/AUTOPILOT/blob/1e0de98/docs/SELF-STUDY/PAPER.md)
- **2026-08-21** — Ninth slice of the human-vs-agent evaluation (backlog J / web-msniol15-foo6oi), the "analyze
  WHERE guidance helps" third of the task title, which no prior slice had touched (slices 1-8 built data planes,
  taxonomy, and trend math — none read the numbers): §5's RQ2 bullet rewritten against the current 1142-firing §4
  snapshot instead of the ~390-firing one it was drafted on (operator-assigned had grown 192 → 739 firings, and the
  evaluation-label table did not exist at all when the old bullet was written). The refreshed reading names where
  the human signal concentrates — admission (42/42 recorded verdicts are proposal-gate filtering) versus execution
  (no operator-vs-agent pass-rate or cost gap once a task is queued) — and adds two capture-artifact caveats the
  label table needs before anyone reads 0.0% approval literally (capture postdates the queued self-proposals'
  approvals; `deleteTask` sweeps record as rejections). Also corrects the free-pick caveat from "selected for
  harder work" to the sharper mechanical fact read directly from `evalRegressionByPickSource`: the bucket is
  `metrics.item IS NULL`, so it counts every instructed NOOP→VERDICT firing and its 0% pass rate measures the
  honest-noop protocol, not failed work. Analysis-only — no code, no new data source; §6's proxy caveat stands
  unchanged. The task itself stays open: shell wiring for checkbox 5 was claimed by a sibling flight this firing,
  and edit/correction capture (checkbox 4's other half) still has no store mechanism to build on.
  - [View the previous version on GitHub](https://github.com/M-A-S-T-E-R-M-I-N-D/AUTOPILOT/blob/fb34671/docs/SELF-STUDY/PAPER.md)
